        var cookieValue =
            document.cookie.replace(/(?:(?:^|.*;\s*)cookie_consent\s*\=\s*((?:[^;](?!;))*[^;]?).*)|.*/, '$1');

        if (cookieValue === 'given') {
            var msg = document.getElementById('cookieBanner');
            if (typeof msg != 'undefined' && msg != null) {
                msg.parentElement.removeChild(msg);
            }
        }

        function acceptCookies() {
            $('#cookieBanner').remove();
            var date = new Date();
            date.setFullYear(date.getFullYear() + 1);

            document.cookie = 'cookie_consent=given; expires=' + date.toGMTString() + '; path=/; domain=britishnewspaperarchive.co.uk';
        }

<!-- Top Banner -->
<section class="banner-container banner-container--beige-background active" id="homepageTopBanner">
<div class="banner">
<button aria-label="Close Banner" class="banner__close-button" id="homepageTopBannerButton" role="button" type="button">
<span class="glyphicon glyphicon-remove"></span>
</button>
<a href="https://www.findmypast.co.uk/ancestry-dna-testing/">
<img class="banner__image banner__image--desktop-only" src="https://findmypast-titan.cdn.prismic.io/findmypast-titan/70e9366595305942610aa8ecc29c6a45826ec32f_bna-dna-homepage-top-banner.png"/>
<img class="banner__image banner__image--mobile-only" src="https://findmypast-titan.cdn.prismic.io/findmypast-titan/dd433eb8757637f002e90fe6ce5342793758465f_bna-dna-homepage-mobile-banner.png"/>
</a>
</div>

<script type="text/javascript">

    (function() {
        var bannerKey = 'HomepageTopBanner';
        var $bannerContainer = $('#homepageTopBanner');
        var _bannerStore = new Persist.Store(bannerKey);
        var hideBanner = null;

        _bannerStore.get(bannerKey, function(ok, value) { hideBanner = value; });

        if (hideBanner != 'hide') {
            $bannerContainer.addClass('active');
        }

        $('#homepageTopBannerButton').one('click',
            function() {
                $bannerContainer.removeClass('active');
                _bannerStore.set(bannerKey, 'hide');
            }
        );
    })();
    
</script>

<section class="bna-search-hero">

<script type="text/javascript">

    (function() {
        var bannerKey = 'HomepageMidBanner';
        var $bannerContainer = $('#homepageMidBanner');
        var _bannerStore = new Persist.Store(bannerKey);
        var hideBanner = null;

        _bannerStore.get(bannerKey, function(ok, value) { hideBanner = value; });

        if (hideBanner != 'hide') {
            $bannerContainer.addClass('active');
        }

        $('#homepageMidBannerButton').one('click',
            function() {
                $bannerContainer.removeClass('active');
                _bannerStore.set(bannerKey, 'hide');
            }
        );
    })();
    
</script>

<h3 class="bna-blog-hero__item__title">
